),
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}=\sum_{l=1}^{L}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{l}
, and

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha}{3\pi-4\mu}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}=\alpha\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}-\frac{1}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha}{3\pi-4\mu}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}-\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

and
w=({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0},\dotsc,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{L})\in\mathcal{C}([0,T],X)
uniquely solves

(26a)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\rho}\mathrm{\,div\,}\Big(\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{l}\Big)+\frac{1}{\rho}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}_{-1},

(26b)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}

\displaystyle=C\big(\mu_{0},\pi_{0}\big)\big(\varepsilon(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}})+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}_{0}\big),

with

.

Remark 4.6
.

Please note that
\Phi^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})^{*}
actually maps into
L^{1}(D)^{5}
which is a subspace of
(L^{\infty}(D)^{5})^{\prime}
. This remark applies also to the adjoints considered in Theorems
4.9
and
4.10
below.

Proof of Theorem
4.5
.

Using
A^{*}=-A
(skew-symmetry),
Q^{*}=Q
, and
QB=BQ
we convince ourselves that (
26
)
is the concrete version of the abstract equation (
12
).
Further, by Theorem
3.3
,

\displaystyle\big\langle\Phi^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})^{*}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}\big\rangle_{(L^{\infty}(D)^{5})^{\prime}\times L^{\infty}(D)^{5}}

\displaystyle=\big\langle F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))^{*}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}\big\rangle_{\mathcal{L}(X)^{\prime}\times\mathcal{L}(X)}

(27)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\!\big\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}\big(u^{\prime}(t)+Qu(t)\big),w(t)\rangle_{X}\,\mathrm{d}t

where

is the classical solution of (

). We are now going to evaluate the above integrand suppressing its

with

and, for

\displaystyle C(\widetilde{\mu},\widetilde{\pi})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi){\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}

(29)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad=\big(2\widetilde{\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}})+(\widetilde{\pi}-2\widetilde{\mu})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}\big):\Big(\frac{1}{2\mu}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\frac{2\mu-\pi}{2\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad=\widetilde{\mu}\Big(\frac{1}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}-\frac{\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0})\Big)+\frac{\widetilde{\pi}}{3\pi-4\mu}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0})

yielding

S_{0}=-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}\\

Analogously,

+\widehat{\mu}\Big(-\frac{1}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{l}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{l})\Big)-\frac{\widehat{\pi}}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{l}).

Next we group the terms in the sum (

(30)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mu}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\mu}{v_{\text{\tiny S}}}\,\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S}}-\frac{\alpha\,\mu}{1+\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\alpha}\,\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S}},

\displaystyle\qquad\widetilde{\pi}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi}{v_{\text{\tiny P}}}\,\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P}}-\frac{\alpha\,\pi}{1+\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\alpha}\,\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}},

(31)

\displaystyle\widehat{\mu}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\,\mu}{v_{\text{\tiny S}}}\,\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S}}+\frac{\mu}{1+\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\alpha}\,\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S}},

\displaystyle\qquad\widehat{\pi}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\,\pi}{v_{\text{\tiny P}}}\,\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P}}+\frac{\pi}{1+\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\alpha}\,\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}.

After some algebra we get

\big\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}\big(u^{\prime}+Qu\big),\overline{u}\rangle_{X}=\int_{D}\Big[\widehat{\rho}\Big(\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}\cdot{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}}-\frac{1}{\rho}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}})\Big)\\
+\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S}}\,\frac{2}{v_{\text{\tiny S}}}\Big(-\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}})+\pi\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v})\mathrm{\,div\,}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}\Big)\\
+\frac{\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S}}}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\Big(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}+\pi\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1})\mathrm{\,div\,}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}\Big)\\
-\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P}}\,\frac{2\pi}{v_{\text{\tiny P}}}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v})\mathrm{\,div\,}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}+\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}\,\frac{\pi}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}})\mathrm{\,div\,}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}\Big]\mathrm{d}x\qquad

which ends the proof.
∎

4.3.
Second order differentiability

The second derivative of
\Phi
is given by

(32)

\Phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]=F^{\prime\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))[V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]+F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$ using the chain and product rules, see, e.g.,
[

, Section 4.3]
. In a first step we need to find

.
Differentiating (

\vdots\\[2.84526pt]

\begin{pmatrix}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{3}}\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}^{\prime}(\mu,\pi)\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\\[2.84526pt]

\widehat{\pi}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\!\\[11.38109pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]

\widehat{\pi}_{2}\end{bmatrix}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{1}\\

\widehat{\pi}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\!\\[11.38109pt]
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{\pi}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{2}\\[2.84526pt]

for

\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{i}=(\widehat{\rho}_{i},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S},i},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},i},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P},i},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},i})\in L^{\infty}(D)^{5}
,

,

, and

,

in (

) and (

\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{i}
. We close the expression
for
V^{\prime\prime}
by

(34)

\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(m,p)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{m}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{p}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{m}_{2}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{p}_{2}\end{bmatrix}=\widetilde{C}(m,p)\circ{C}(\widehat{m}_{1},\widehat{p}_{1})\circ\widetilde{C}(m,p)\circ{C}(\widehat{m}_{2},\widehat{p}_{2})\circ\widetilde{C}(m,p)\\
+\widetilde{C}(m,p)\circ{C}(\widehat{m}_{2},\widehat{p}_{2})\circ\widetilde{C}(m,p)\circ{C}(\widehat{m}_{1},\widehat{p}_{1})\circ\widetilde{C}(m,p).

The proof of (
34
) requires straightforward but lengthy calculations.

Theorem 4.7
.

Let
\textstyle\mathbf{f}
be in
W^{3,1}([0,T],L^{2}(D,\mathbb{R}^{3}))
with
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{f}$}}(0)=\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{f}$}}^{\prime}(0)=\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{f}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{f}$}}^{\prime\prime}(0)=0
. Further, let
u_{0}=0
and adopt the assumptions and notation made in this section.

Then, the
full waveform forward operator

\Phi
is twice Fréchet differentiable at any interior point

\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}=(\rho,v_{\text{\tiny S}},\tau_{\text{\tiny S}},v_{\text{\tiny P}},\tau_{\text{\tiny P}})
of
\mathsf{D}(\Phi)
: For

\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{i}=(\widehat{\rho}_{i},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S},i},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S}.i},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P},i},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},i})\in L^{\infty}(D)^{5}
,
i=1,2
, we have
\Phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]={v}+\overline{\overline{u}}
where
{v}=(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{0},\dotsc,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{L})
and

\overline{\overline{u}}=(\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}},\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}}_{0},\dotsc,\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}}_{L})
are both in
\mathcal{C}([0,T],X)
. They are uniquely determined as mild solutions of the following viscoelastic
equations.

The equations for
\overline{\overline{u}}
are
\overline{\overline{u}}(0)=0
and

\displaystyle\rho\,\partial_{t}\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{\,div\,}\Big(\sum_{l=0}^{L}\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}}_{l}\Big)-\widehat{\rho}_{1}\,\partial_{t}\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{2}-\widehat{\rho}_{2}\,\partial_{t}\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{1},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}}_{0}

\displaystyle=C(\mu_{0},\pi_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}})+\big(\widehat{\rho}_{1}\,C(\mu,\pi)+\rho\,C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\big(\widehat{\rho}_{2}\,C(\mu,\pi)+\rho\,C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{1}),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}}_{l}

\displaystyle=C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu_{0},\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}})-\frac{1}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},l}}\,\overline{\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}}_{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\big(\widehat{\rho}_{1}\,C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)+\rho\,C(\widehat{\mu}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{1})\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\big(\widehat{\rho}_{2}\,C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)+\rho\,C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{1}),\quad l=1,\dotsc,L,

with
\overline{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{i}
being the first component of the solution of
(
25
) where the parameters
\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}
have to be replaced by
\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{i}
,
i=1,2
.

The equations for
v
are
v(0)=0
and

\displaystyle\rho\,\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{\,div\,}\Big(\sum_{l=0}^{L}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{l}\Big),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle=C(\mu_{0},\pi_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}})-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}C(\mu,\pi)+\widehat{\rho}_{1}C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})+\widehat{\rho}_{2}C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\rho C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})+\rho C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{l}

\displaystyle=C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu_{0},\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}})-\frac{1}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},l}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{l}-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)+\widehat{\rho}_{1}C(\widehat{\mu}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{1})+\widehat{\rho}_{2}C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\rho C(\widehat{\mu}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{1})\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})+\rho C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)C(\widehat{\mu}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{1})\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}),

l=1,\dotsc,L
, where
\textstyle\mathbf{v}
is the first component of the solution of (
4
).

Proof.

By (
32
),
\Phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]=v+\overline{\overline{u}}
where

v:=F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]\quad\text{and}\quad\overline{\overline{u}}:=F^{\prime\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))[V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}].

We apply Theorems
3.2
and
3.5
to specify the equations for
v
and

\overline{\overline{u}}
, respectively.

We start with
\overline{\overline{u}}
which is determined by two coupled equations of type (
25
). These equations only differ in the plugged in parameters and right hand sides.

Theorem
3.2
yields the following system for
v
:

\begin{pmatrix}{\rho}\,\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\rho}\,\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{0}\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\rho}\,\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\vdots\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\rho}\,\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{L}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{\,div\,}\big(\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}}_{l}\big)\\[2.84526pt]
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}})\\[2.84526pt]
\vdots\\[2.84526pt]
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}})\end{pmatrix}-\begin{pmatrix}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\rho\,\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},1}}\,\widetilde{C}\big(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\vdots\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\rho\,\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},L}}\,\widetilde{C}\big(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{L}\end{pmatrix}\\
-V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]\left[\begin{pmatrix}\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}_{0}\\[2.84526pt]
\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\vdots\\[2.84526pt]
\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}_{L}\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{0}$}}\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},1}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\vdots\\[2.84526pt]
\frac{1}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},L}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}_{L}\end{pmatrix}\right]\!.

Applying (
4b
), (
4c
), (
21
), (
33
), and (
34
) leads to the equations for

v
.
∎

4.4.
An additional adjoint

As explained in the introduction, second-degree Newton solvers might resolve the cross-talk effect.
In our group we plan to implement a variant of the second-degree Newton method for viscolesatic FWI. There one needs to solve a linear system containing
the operator
\Phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},\cdot]
. Our regularization method of choice is the conjugate gradient iteration
which needs the adjoint operator. In this subsection we derive an explicit expression for it.

Recall from (
32
) that

(35)

\Phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},\cdot]=F^{\prime\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))[V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\,\cdot]+F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},\cdot].

In a first step we therefore consider
F^{\prime\prime}(B)[H,\cdot]\colon\mathcal{L}^{*}(X)\to L^{2}([0,T],X)
for
B\in\mathsf{D}(F)
and

H\in\mathcal{L}^{*}(X)
.

Theorem 4.8
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
3.5
we have

\big[F^{\prime\prime}({B})[H_{1},\cdot]^{\ast}g\big]H_{2}=\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\big\langle H_{1}({u}_{2}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{2}(t))+H_{2}({u}_{1}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{1}(t)),w(t)\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t

for
g\in L^{2}([0,T],X)
,
H_{i}\in\mathcal{L}^{*}(X)
, where
{u}_{i}=F^{\prime}(B)H_{i}
,
i=1,2
, is the solution of (
14
). Further,

w\in\mathcal{C}([0,T],X)
is the mild solution of the
adjoint
evolution equation

{B}w^{\prime}(t)-A^{\ast}w(t)-Q^{*}Bw(t)=g(t),\ t\in\,[0,T],\quad w(T)=0.

Proof.

Since
\big[F^{\prime\prime}({B})[H_{1},\cdot]^{\ast}g\big]H_{2}=\langle\overline{\overline{u}},g\rangle_{L^{2}([0,T],X)}

where
\overline{\overline{u}}\in\mathcal{C}^{1}([0,T],X)
solves (
13
) we argue similar to the proof of Theorem 3.8 in
[
7
]
:

Assume
g\in W^{1,1}([0,T],X)
. Then,
w\in\mathcal{C}^{1}([0,T],X)
. Using the selfadjointness of
B
and integration by parts we compute

\displaystyle\langle\overline{\overline{u}},g\rangle_{L^{2}([0,T],X)}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\langle\overline{\overline{u}}(t),g(t)\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t=\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\langle\overline{\overline{u}}(t),{B}w^{\prime}(t)-A^{\ast}w(t)-Q^{*}Bw(t)\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\langle B\overline{\overline{u}}\,^{\prime}(t)+A\overline{\overline{u}}(t)+BQ\overline{\overline{u}}(t),w(t)\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\!\!\big\langle H_{1}({u}_{2}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{2}(t))+H_{2}({u}_{1}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{1}(t)),w(t)\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t.

The assertion follows since
W^{1,1}([0,T],X)
is dense in
L^{2}([0,T],X)
.

∎

Theorem 4.9
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
4.7
we have that
the adjoint

F^{\prime\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))[V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\,\cdot]^{*}\in\mathcal{L}\big(L^{2}([0,T],X),(L^{\infty}(D)^{5})^{\prime}\big)

at
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}=(\rho,v_{\text{\tiny S}},\tau_{\text{\tiny S}},v_{\text{\tiny P}},\tau_{\text{\tiny P}})\in\mathsf{D}(\Phi)
and
\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}=(\widehat{\rho},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S}},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S}},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P}},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}})\in L^{\infty}(D)^{5}
is given by

F^{\prime\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))[V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\cdot]^{*}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}=\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}_{1}^{*}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}_{2}^{*}

for
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}=(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}_{-1},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}_{0},\dotsc,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}_{L})\in L^{2}\big([0,T],L^{2}(D,\mathbb{R}^{3})\times L^{2}(D,\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}_{\rm sym})^{1+L}\big)

where

\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}_{1}^{*}=\begin{pmatrix}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\partial_{t}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}\cdot{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{z}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{z}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{z}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{z}$}}}-\frac{1}{\rho}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}):({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}}_{0}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\eta})\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{2}{v_{\text{\tiny S}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(-\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}):({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}}_{0}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\eta})+\pi\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{1}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}+\pi\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
-\frac{2\pi}{v_{\text{\tiny P}}}\int_{0}^{T}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}\,\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{\pi}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}\int_{0}^{T}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}\,\mathrm{d}t\end{pmatrix}\!\!\in\!L^{1}(D)^{5}

and

\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}_{2}^{*}=\begin{pmatrix}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\partial_{t}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{1}\cdot{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}}-\frac{1}{\rho}\big[\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi})+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}_{1}^{\rho}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}_{2}^{\rho})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big]\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{2}{v_{\text{\tiny S}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(-\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi})-\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}_{1}^{\rho}\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},1})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{1}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(-\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{1}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi})-\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}_{\text{\tiny S},0}^{\tau}\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
-\frac{2\pi}{v_{\text{\tiny P}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P},1})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P},2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{\pi}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},1})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}-\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\end{pmatrix}\!\!\in\!L^{1}(D)^{5}.

Above,
\textstyle\mathbf{v}
is the first component of the solution of (
4
),

z=({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{z}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{z}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{z}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{z}$}}},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}}_{0},\dotsc,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}}_{L})
solves (
12
) with
g=-V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}(w^{\prime}+Qw)
and
w=(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}},{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0},\dotsc,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{L})
solves (

).
Further,

\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\eta}=\sum_{l=1}^{L}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}_{l}
,
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi}=\sum_{l=1}^{L}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}
, and the quantities

\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v}
,
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}
,

, and

are exactly those from Theorem
