.
Finally,
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}=(F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}})_{1}
, that is,
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}
is the first component of the solution of (
25
), and

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\rho}_{1}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\rho}_{2}=\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}-\widetilde{\mu}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu\widehat{\pi}-\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\widehat{\mu}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{3\pi-4\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi}

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{3\pi-4\mu}-\frac{3\widetilde{\mu}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}\mu^{2}}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}-\frac{3\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},0}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\rho}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\frac{\pi}{3\pi-4\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{3\pi-4\mu}-\frac{3\widetilde{\mu}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}\mu^{2}}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}-\frac{3\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P},2}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}+\frac{3\widetilde{\pi}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+\frac{3\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha}{3\pi-4\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}-\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},2}

\displaystyle=\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}+\frac{3\widetilde{\pi}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+\frac{3\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi}.

Proof.

From the previous theorem we have that

\displaystyle\big(F^{\prime\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))[V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\,\cdot]^{*}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}\big)\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1}({u}_{2}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{2}(t)),w(t)\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{T}\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}({u}_{1}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{1}(t)),w(t)\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t.

Note that here
{u}_{i}=F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{i}
solves (
25
) with
\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}=\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{i}
and
w
solves (
26
).

We start with

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1}({u}_{2}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}_{2}(t)),w(t)\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\langle u_{2},V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1}(w^{\prime}(t)+Qw(t))\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}({u}^{\prime}(t)+Q{u}(t)),z(t)\big\rangle_{X}\mathrm{d}t.

We are now exactly in the situation of the proof of Theorem
4.5
, see (
27
),
and proceed accordingly which yields the representation of
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}_{1}^{*}
.

Similar to (
28
) we find
for
\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}=(\widehat{\rho}_{2},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P},2},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},2})
that

\big\langle V^{\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}\big(u_{1}^{\prime}+Qu_{1}\big),w\rangle_{X}=\int_{D}\big(\widehat{\rho}_{2}\,\partial_{t}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}\cdot{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{w}$}}}+S_{0}+S_{1}+\cdots+S_{L}\big)\mathrm{d}x

with

S_{0}=\Big[-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{0,1}-\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{0,1}\Big]:{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}

and, for
l=1,\dotsc,L
,

S_{l}=\Big[-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\Big(\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l,1}+\frac{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l,1}}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},l}}\Big)\\
-\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\Big(\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l,1}+\frac{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l,1}}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},l}}\Big)\Big]:{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{l}.

In view of (
25b
) and (
25c
) we have that

\partial_{t}{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\sigma}$}}}_{0,1}=C(\mu_{0},\pi_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{1})+\big(\widehat{\rho}_{1}\,C(\mu,\pi)+\rho\,C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}})

and

\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l,1}+\frac{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l,1}}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},l}}=C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu_{0},\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}}_{1})+\big(\widehat{\rho}_{1}\,C(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)+\rho\,C(\widehat{\mu}_{1}\widehat{\pi}_{1})\big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}),\quad l=1,\dotsc,L,

which we plug into
S_{0}
and
S_{l}
, respectively. Thus,

S_{0}=-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}-C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\script -\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho}C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}\\
-\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

and

S_{l}=-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}-C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1}):\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}\\
-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho}C(\widehat{\mu}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}\\
-\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)C(\widehat{\mu}_{1},\widehat{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):C(\widehat{\mu}_{2},\widehat{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}.

We have

\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v})=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v})+\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}_{1}-\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}

and

C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{2}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}_{2}-\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}

so that

\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\widetilde{\mu}_{2}}{\mu^{2}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}\\
+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{2}(3\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu^{2})+\widetilde{\pi}_{2}(3\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\mu^{2})}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}).

Plugging these identities into

(and an adapted version into

) and recalling (

\displaystyle S_{0}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\rho}_{2}\Bigg(-\frac{1}{\rho}\Big[\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1})+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}})\Big]:\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{1}{\rho}\Big(\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}-\frac{\widetilde{\pi}_{1}}{3\pi-4\mu}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\Bigg(-\frac{1}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}_{1}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho\mu}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\mu^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}+\frac{3\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\pi}_{2}\Bigg(\frac{-1}{3\pi-4\mu}\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}-\frac{3\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

and

\displaystyle S_{l}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\rho}_{2}\Bigg(-\frac{1}{\rho}\Big[\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}_{1})+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}})\Big]:\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\rho}\Big(\frac{\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}-\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{1}}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\mu}_{2}\Bigg(-\frac{1}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}_{1}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}+\frac{3\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\pi}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\pi}_{2}\Bigg(\frac{-1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}_{1}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}-\frac{3\widehat{\pi}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg).

Theorem 4.10
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
4.7
we have that
the adjoint

F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},\cdot]^{*}\in\mathcal{L}\big(L^{2}([0,T],X),(L^{\infty}(D)^{5})^{\prime}\big)

at
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}=(\rho,v_{\text{\tiny S}},\tau_{\text{\tiny S}},v_{\text{\tiny P}},\tau_{\text{\tiny P}})\in\mathsf{D}(\Phi)
and
\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}=(\widehat{\rho},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny S}},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S}},\widehat{v}_{\text{\tiny P}},\widehat{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}})\in L^{\infty}(D)^{5}
is given by

F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},\cdot]^{*}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}=\begin{pmatrix}\frac{1}{\rho}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\rho}_{1}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\rho}_{2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{2}{v_{\text{\tiny S}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},1}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]
\frac{1}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\int_{0}^{T}\big(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}+\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\big)\mathrm{d}t\\[5.69054pt]

\frac{\pi}{1+\alpha\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}\int_{0}^{T}\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\mathrm{d}t\end{pmatrix}\in L^{1}(D)^{5}

for

where

\textstyle\mathbf{v}
is the first component of the solution of (

). Let

) with

and set
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}=\sum_{l=1}^{L}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\rho}_{1}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\rho}_{2}=\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}-\widetilde{\mu}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu\widehat{\pi}-\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\widehat{\mu}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{2\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2}

\displaystyle=\Big(2\,\frac{3\widetilde{\mu}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}\mu^{2}}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{3\pi-4\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(2\,\frac{3\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\rho}+\frac{2\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(2\,\frac{3\widetilde{\mu}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}\mu^{2}}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{3\pi-4\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(2\,\frac{3\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P}}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}+2\,\frac{3\widetilde{\pi}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+2\,\frac{3\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\pi-4\mu}+2\,\frac{3\widetilde{\pi}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+2\,\frac{3\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

with the abbreviations

.

Proof.

Since

\big(F^{\prime}(V(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}))V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\cdot]^{*}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{g}$}}\big)\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:aux_FWI_ad}}}{{=}}\int_{0}^{T}\!\big\langle V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]\big(u^{\prime}(t)+Qu(t)\big),w(t)\rangle_{X}\,\mathrm{d}t.

we are basically again in the situation of the proof of Theorem

. Using (

) we find that

\big\langle V^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{1},\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}}_{2}]\big(u^{\prime}+Qu\big),w\rangle_{X}=\int_{D}\big(S_{0}+S_{1}+\cdots+S_{L}\big)\mathrm{d}x

with

S_{0}=\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{3}}\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}^{\prime}(\mu,\pi)\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\\[2.84526pt]
\widetilde{\pi}_{2}\end{bmatrix}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}^{\prime}(\mu,\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\\
+\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(\mu,\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]

\widetilde{\pi}_{2}\end{bmatrix}\Bigg)\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{0}:\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}

and

S_{l}=\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{3}}\widetilde{C}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}^{\prime}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{2}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{\pi}_{2}\end{bmatrix}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\widetilde{C}^{\prime}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{\pi}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\\
+\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu,\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{\pi}_{1}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\mu}_{2}\\[2.84526pt]
\widehat{\pi}_{2}\end{bmatrix}\Bigg)\Big(\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l}+\frac{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{l}}{\tau_{{\boldsymbol{\sigma}},l}}\Big):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\psi}$}}_{l},\quad l=1,\dotsc,L.

First we simplify
S_{0}
. By (

\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{0}:\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}=\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}.

Further, in view of (

-\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime}(\mu,\pi)\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{i}\\[2.84526pt]
\widetilde{\pi}_{i}\end{bmatrix}\partial_{t}{\boldsymbol{\sigma}}_{0}:\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}\\
=\widetilde{\mu}_{i}\Big(\frac{1}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}-\frac{\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0})\Big)+\frac{\widetilde{\pi}_{i}}{3\pi-4\mu}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}),\quad i=1,2.

) and (

\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(\mu,\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]

+\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}.

We have

\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v})=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{2}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v})+\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}_{2}-\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}

and

so that

\frac{1}{\rho}\widetilde{C}^{\prime\prime}(\mu,\pi)\!\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\\[2.84526pt]

+2\,\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{2}(3\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu^{2})+\widetilde{\pi}_{2}(3\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\mu^{2})}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}).

Substituting above auxiliary results into the expression for

yields

\displaystyle S_{0}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\rho}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\rho\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{1}{\rho}\Big(\frac{\widetilde{\pi}_{1}}{3\pi-4\mu}-\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\Big)\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho\mu}+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\mu^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(2\,\frac{3\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi^{2}-4\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\pi}_{2}\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{\mu(3\pi-4\mu)}+2\,\frac{3\widetilde{\pi}_{1}-4\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{(3\pi-4\mu)^{2}}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}).

based on (

\displaystyle S_{l}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\rho}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{\rho\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}+\frac{1}{\rho}\Big(\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{1}}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}-\frac{\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\Big)\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\mu}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+\frac{2\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\Big(2\,\frac{3\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-4\widehat{\pi}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg)

Next we replace

by their values

In view of (
