) we have now derived an analytic expression for
\Phi^{\prime\prime}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}})[\widehat{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}},\cdot]^{*}
in rather basic terms.

Appendix A
Two spatial dimensions

The expressions for the Fréchet derivatives and their adjoints provided in the main part of this paper cannot directly be applied to
the viscoelastic wave equation in two spatial dimensions. The differences to the 3D case which have to be taken into account are

\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}})=2\quad\text{and}\quad\widetilde{C}(m,p)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}=C^{-1}(m,p)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}=\frac{1}{2m}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}+\frac{2m-p}{4m(p-m)}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{M}$}})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}.

With these ingredients the derivatives and adjoints can be
calculated exactly along the lines presented on the previous pages.

In this appendix we provide 2D versions of
Theorems
4.5
,
4.9
, and
4.10
.

Theorem A.1

(2D version of Theorem
4.5
)
.

The only quantities which have to be changed are
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v}
,
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}
,
and
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}
. With

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\alpha}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}{2\,\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}-\frac{1}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

the statement of Theorem
4.5
can be copied without any further changes.

Proof.

The only difference to the 3D proof concerns the computation of, compare (
29
),

\displaystyle C(\widetilde{\mu},\widetilde{\pi})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi){\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad=\big(2\widetilde{\mu}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}})+(\widetilde{\pi}-2\widetilde{\mu})\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}\big):\Big(\frac{1}{2\mu}\,{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}+\frac{2\mu-\pi}{4\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad=\widetilde{\mu}\Big(\frac{1}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):{\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}-\frac{\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0})\Big)+\frac{\widetilde{\pi}}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}({\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}_{0}).

∎

Theorem A.2

(2D version of Theorem
4.9
)
.

The expression for
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}^{*}_{1}
in Theorem
4.9
remains correct for the
2D
case when the
2D
versions of
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{v}
,
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}
,
and
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}
from the above theorem are taken (with
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\eta}$}}_{l}
in place of
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}
).
In the expression for
\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{p}$}}^{*}_{2}
we need to redefine the following quantities:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\rho}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}-\widetilde{\mu}\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu\widehat{\pi}-\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\widehat{\mu}\pi}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{2(\pi-\mu)}-\mathrm{K}_{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}-\mathrm{K}_{\mu,\tau}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\frac{\pi}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{2(\pi-\mu)}-\mathrm{K}_{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}-\frac{\mathrm{K}_{\mu,\tau}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P},2}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\pi-\mu)}+\mathrm{K}_{\pi}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{3\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-4\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+\mathrm{K}_{\pi,\tau}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha}{2(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}-\frac{1}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Gamma}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P},2}

\displaystyle=\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\pi-\mu)}+\mathrm{K}_{\pi}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}+\mathrm{K}_{\pi,\tau}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}_{\varphi}.

where

\displaystyle\mathrm{K}_{\mu}=\frac{2\pi\mu\widetilde{\mu}-\widetilde{\mu}\pi^{2}-\widetilde{\pi}\mu^{2}}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)^{2}},\quad\mathrm{K}_{\mu,\tau}=\frac{2\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu\widehat{\mu}-\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathrm{K}_{\pi}=\frac{\widetilde{\pi}-\widetilde{\mu}}{2(\pi-\mu)^{2}},\quad\mathrm{K}_{\pi,\tau}=\frac{\widehat{\pi}-\widehat{\mu}}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}.

Proof.

We have

\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v})=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v})+\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}_{1}-\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}

and

C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{2}}{\mu}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\mu\widetilde{\pi}_{2}-\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{I}$}}

so that

\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)C(\widetilde{\mu}_{1},\widetilde{\pi}_{1})\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{v}$}}):C(\widetilde{\mu}_{2},\widetilde{\pi}_{2})\widetilde{C}(\mu,\pi)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}=\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\widetilde{\mu}_{2}}{\mu^{2}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}\\
+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{2}(2\pi\mu\widetilde{\mu}_{1}-\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi^{2}-\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu^{2})+\widetilde{\pi}_{2}\mu^{2}(\widetilde{\pi}_{1}-\widetilde{\mu}_{1})}{2\mu^{2}(\pi-\mu)^{2}}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}).

For the next steps see the proof of Theorem
4.9
.
∎

Theorem A.3

(2D version of Theorem
4.10
)
.

Theorem
4.10
remains correct for the
2D
case when the definitions of the
\Upsilon
’s are replaced by

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\rho}_{1}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\rho}_{2}=\frac{\widetilde{\pi}\mu-{\widetilde{\mu}}\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu-{\widehat{\mu}}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{2\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny S},2}=\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny S},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny S},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},1}

\displaystyle=-\alpha\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}}{\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\rho}+\frac{2\widehat{\mu}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny S},2}=-\alpha\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny S},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny S},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}/\tau_{\text{\tiny S}},

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{v}_{\text{\tiny P}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny P},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\,\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny P},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},\quad\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Upsilon}$}}^{\tau}_{\text{\tiny P}}=-\alpha\,\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny P},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny P},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}}\,\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}},

where

\displaystyle\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny S},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi\mu\widetilde{\mu}-\widetilde{\mu}\pi^{2}-\widetilde{\pi}\mu^{2}}{\mu(\pi-\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{2(\pi-\mu)},

\displaystyle\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny S},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu\widehat{\mu}-\widehat{\mu}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-\widehat{\pi}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)},

\displaystyle\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny P},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\pi-\mu)}+\frac{\widetilde{\pi}-\widetilde{\mu}}{(\pi-\mu)^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathrm{K}_{\text{\tiny P},\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\Sigma}$}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\rho}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}+\frac{\widehat{\pi}-\widehat{\mu}}{(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}.

Proof.

Let
S_{0}
and
S_{l}
,
l=1,\dotsc,L
, be defined as in the proof of Theorem
4.10
. Then, taking into account
the formulas provided in the previous two proofs,

\displaystyle S_{0}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\rho}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\rho\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\frac{1}{\rho}\,\frac{\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu-\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\mu}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho\mu}+\frac{2\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{\mu^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0}+\Big(\frac{2\pi\mu\widetilde{\mu}_{1}-\widetilde{\mu}_{1}\pi^{2}-\widetilde{\pi}_{1}\mu^{2}}{\mu^{2}(\pi-\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{\pi}{2\mu(\pi-\mu)}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widetilde{\pi}_{2}\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\pi-\mu)}+\frac{\widetilde{\pi}_{1}-\widetilde{\mu}_{1}}{(\pi-\mu)^{2}}\Bigg)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{0})

and

\displaystyle S_{l}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\rho}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{\rho\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}+\frac{1}{\rho}\,\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu-\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\,\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\mu}_{2}\Bigg(\Big(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu}+\frac{2\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}\Big)\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varepsilon}$}}({\bf v}):\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\Big(\frac{2\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu\widehat{\mu}_{1}-\widehat{\mu}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}^{2}\pi^{2}-\widehat{\pi}_{1}\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}}{\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}^{2}\mu^{2}(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}-\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi}{2\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}\Big)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\pi}_{2}\Bigg(\frac{\widehat{\rho}_{1}}{\rho}\,\frac{1}{2(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)}+\frac{\widehat{\pi}_{1}-\widehat{\mu}_{1}}{(\tau_{\text{\tiny P}}\pi-\tau_{\text{\tiny S}}\mu)^{2}}\Bigg)\mathrm{\,div\,}{\bf v}\,\operatorname{tr}(\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbf{\varphi}$}}_{l}).

The next steps are as in the proof of Theorem
4.10
.
∎
